📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 三尖杉酯碱与低剂量顺铂联合使用时可作为化疗增敏剂。这种效应在人乳头瘤病毒阳性细胞中最为显著。人乳头瘤病毒阳性细胞还表现出TP53启动子活性增加和TP53蛋白磷酸化增强。STRING分析显示,人乳头瘤病毒阳性样本中涉及JAK-STAT信号通路、白细胞介素-23信号通路和细胞凋亡通路。在人乳头瘤病毒阴性样本中,三尖杉酯碱富集的目标与RUNX3调控CDKN1A转录以及白细胞介素-4、白细胞介素-13和白细胞介素-17信号通路相关。
  • 本研究共纳入204,196名未接种疫苗的青少年,其中66.0%的家长被归类为HPV 疫苗犹豫。总体而言,母亲被归类为HPV 疫苗犹豫的青少年比例从2010年的72%下降到2022年的61%。所有种族和民族背景的家长在2010年至2012年间都经历了HPV 疫苗犹豫率的显著下降,之后在2012年至2022年间保持稳定。
  • 人乳头瘤病毒 6型和11型与黏膜尖锐湿疣相关,外生殖器表现罕见。 [10.3201/eid3210.260872]。
  • 在94例检测到HPV16的肿瘤中,83例属于HPV16_A1亚型。在该亚型中,20例肿瘤仅含有严格的游离病毒基因组,而63例则表现出整合和/或病毒结构变异。在严格的游离HPV16_A1亚组中未观察到任何进展事件,而在存在整合和/或结构变异的HPV16_A1肿瘤中观察到12例进展事件。在60个月随访时,严格的游离HPV16_A1肿瘤未观察到进展事件,而存在整合和/或结构变异的HPV16_A1肿瘤的进展累积概率为23.1%。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Triptolide acts as a chemosensitizer in combination with low-dose cisplatin. This effect is most pronounced in HPV-positive cells.
  • A total of 204,196 unvaccinated adolescents were included in the study, 66.0% had parents who were classified as HPV vaccine hesitant.
  • Human papillomavirus types 6 and 11 are associated with mucosal condylomata acuminata, with rare reports of extragenital manifestations. [10.3201/eid3210.260872].
  • HPV16 was detected in 94 tumors, 83 of which belonged to the HPV16_A1 sublineage. Within this sublineage, 20 tumors harbored strictly episomal viral genomes, whereas 63 exhibited integration and/or viral SV.

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • Dare Bioscience宣布获得一项新的美国专利,该专利覆盖用于治疗与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关的宫颈疾病的局部洛匹那韦和利托那韦制剂。该公司正在开发DARE-HPV,作为潜在的首个获得FDA批准的抗病毒药物治疗用于治疗HPV感染。

  • Dare Bioscience announced a newly issued U.S. patent covering topical lopinavir and ritonavir formulations for HPV-related cervical conditions.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of the American Dental Association (1939)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-29  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.adaj.2026.07.009  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, S0002-8177(26)00460-5

人乳头瘤病毒在口腔和口咽癌中的致癌作用:分子机制与牙科实践的临床意义

Oncogenic role of human papillomavirus in oral and oropharyngeal cancers: Molecular insights and clinical implications for dental practice.
作者： Chintan Desai, Bharani Kumar Bhattu, Radhika Narayan, Kaushal Chaudhari, Abhi Thakkar
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)阳性口咽部鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC)已成为头颈恶性肿瘤中一个独特且日益增多的亚型,但牙科护理人员对此的认知仍较为有限。作者综合了HPV驱动的口腔及口咽部癌变的分子生物学机制、诊断框架以及支持扩大牙科护理人员在检测、咨询和预防方面作用的证据基础。回顾的研究类型:通过结构化的文献检索,使用包括HPV、口咽癌、口腔鳞状细胞癌、p16免疫组化、HPV 疫苗接种、以及牙科实践等关键词,在PubMed索引的英文文章中进行了至2026年3月的搜索。选取了原始研究、系统综述、临床指南和人群层面的监测数据,特别关注在牙科环境中具有临床、诊断或预防结果的研究。作者未纳入单个病例报告或小病例系列,并排除了会议摘要、社论以及非英文文章。结果:HPV 16型通过E6介导的p53降解和E7介导的视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白失活,驱动了70%至90%的HPV阳性OPSCC。HPV阳性OPSCC的预后优于HPV阴性疾病,但缺乏可临床识别的癌前阶段,通常表现为局部晚期病变。p16免疫组化在口腔和以烟草为主的群体中特异性较低。预防性HPV 疫苗接种在接种疫苗的年轻成人中,将疫苗型口腔HPV 患病率减少了88%。结论与实际意义:牙科护理人员可以通过系统的临床检查、颈部淋巴结触诊、及时转诊专科医生、对HPV 传播进行患者咨询以及推动疫苗接种的倡导,有意义地促进早期检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决牙科从业人员对HPV阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌的认知不足问题,该疾病是头颈恶性肿瘤中一个独特且日益增多的亚型。
设计与对象
通过2026年3月的PubMed索引英文文章进行结构化文献检索,重点关注在牙科环境中具有临床、诊断或预防结果的研究。
核心方法
作者回顾了原始研究、系统综述、临床指南和人口水平的监测数据,排除了单个病例报告、小病例系列和非英文文章。
主要结果
HPV 16型通过E6介导的p53降解和E7介导的视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白失活,导致约70%至90%的HPV阳性口咽癌(OPSCC)。HPV阳性OPSCC的预后优于HPV阴性疾病,但缺乏可临床识别的癌前阶段,通常表现为局部晚期病变。P16免疫组化在口腔和烟草主导人群中特异性降低。
解释与创新
作者认为HPV16是HPV阳性OPSCC的主要驱动因素,并指出牙科从业人员可以通过系统的临床检查、颈部淋巴结触诊、及时转诊专科医生、对HPV 传播进行患者咨询以及推动疫苗接种,有意义地促进早期检测。
科研与公卫意义
牙科从业人员可以在HPV阳性OPSCC的检测、咨询和预防中发挥重要作用,而HPV阳性OPSCC的预后优于HPV阴性疾病。
局限与证据强度
研究的局限性包括排除了单个病例报告和小病例系列,以及依赖英文文章。由于采用了结构化文献检索并纳入了多种类型的研究,证据强度中等。

Oncogenic role of human papillomavirus in oral and oropharyngeal cancers: Molecular insights and clinical implications for dental practice.

Authors: Chintan Desai, Bharani Kumar Bhattu, Radhika Narayan, Kaushal Chaudhari, Abhi Thakkar
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV)-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) has emerged as a distinct and rising subset of head and neck malignancy, yet awareness among dental care practitioners remains limited. The authors synthesized the molecular biology of HPV-driven oral and oropharyngeal carcinogenesis, the diagnostic frameworks, and the evidence base for an expanded dental care practitioner role in detection, counseling, and prevention. TYPES OF STUDIES REVIEWED: A structured literature search of PubMed-indexed English-language articles through March 2026 was conducted using search terms including HPV, oropharyngeal cancer, oral squamous cell carcinoma, p16 immunohistochemistry, HPV vaccination, and dental practice. Original research, systematic reviews, clinical guidelines, and population-level surveillance data were selected, with an emphasis on studies reporting clinical, diagnostic, or prevention outcomes applicable to dental settings. The authors did not include single case reports or small case series, and they excluded conference abstracts, editorials, and articles not available in the English language. RESULTS: HPV type 16 drives from 70% through 90% of HPV-positive OPSCCs through E6-mediated p53 degradation and E7-mediated retinoblastoma protein inactivation. HPV-positive OPSCC carries a better prognosis than HPV-negative disease but lacks a clinically identifiable premalignant phase and typically manifests at an advanced locoregional stage. P16 immunohistochemistry has reduced specificity in the oral cavity and tobacco-predominant populations. Prophylactic HPV vaccination had an 88% reduction in vaccine-type oral HPV prevalence among vaccinated young adults. CONCLUSIONS AND PRACTICAL IMPLICATIONS: Dental care practitioners can contribute meaningfully to earlier detection through systematic clinical examination, cervical lymph node palpation, timely specialist referral, patient counseling on HPV transmission, and vaccination advocacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited awareness among dental care practitioners regarding HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma, a distinct and rising subset of head and neck malignancy.
Design and population
A structured literature search of PubMed-indexed English-language articles through March 2026 was conducted, focusing on studies reporting clinical, diagnostic, or prevention outcomes applicable to dental settings.
Core methods
The authors reviewed original research, systematic reviews, clinical guidelines, and population-level surveillance data, excluding single case reports, small case series, and non-English language articles.
Main results
HPV type 16 drives from 70% through 90% of HPV-positive OPSCCs through E6-mediated p53 degradation and E7-mediated retinoblastoma protein inactivation. HPV-positive OPSCC carries a better prognosis than HPV-negative disease but lacks a clinically identifiable premalignant phase and typically manifests at an advanced locoregional stage. P16 immunohistochemistry has reduced specificity in the oral cavity and tobacco-predominant populations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HPV16 is the predominant driver of HPV-positive OPSCCs and that dental care practitioners can contribute to earlier detection through systematic clinical examination, cervical lymph node palpation, timely specialist referral, patient counseling on HPV transmission, and vaccination advocacy.
Scientific and public-health significance
Dental care practitioners can play a meaningful role in the detection, counseling, and prevention of HPV-positive OPSCCs, which have a better prognosis than HPV-negative disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study limitations include the exclusion of single case reports and small case series, and the reliance on English-language articles. The evidence strength is moderate due to the structured literature search and the inclusion of various types of studies.

通过TP53,三尖杉酯碱作为人乳头瘤病毒头颈鳞状细胞癌的化学增敏剂

Triptolide as a Chemosensitizer in Human Papillomavirus Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma Through TP53.
作者： WILLIAM KAMM, Q I Zhang, LINDSEY MORTENSEN, BEVERLY WUERTZ, FRANK G. ONDREY
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)是全球第七常见的癌症,近年来其病因学发生了显著变化。人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)感染是HNSCC的关键风险因素,其发病率自1980年以来持续上升。与HPV相关的HNSCC表现出独特的肿瘤发生过程,并对传统的HNSCC 治疗治疗反应不同。三尖杉酯碱作为一种天然衍生物,已被证明在HPV阳性HNSCC中具有作为化学增敏剂的潜力。本研究旨在通过体外实验评估三尖杉酯碱在HNSCC中的活性,并结合三尖杉酯碱基因靶点的计算机模拟分析。材料与方法:通过体外MTT实验评估三尖杉酯碱对HPV阳性及HPV阴性HNSCC细胞增殖的影响。通过报告基因实验和Western blot分别检测三尖杉酯碱对TP53启动子活性及TP53蛋白磷酸化的影响。随后,通过cBioPortal和癌症基因组图谱(TCGA)评估三尖杉酯碱基因靶点的富集情况,并进行STRING分析。结果:三尖杉酯碱在与低剂量顺铂联合使用时表现出化学增敏作用,这种作用在HPV阳性细胞中最为显著。HPV阳性细胞还显示出TP53启动子活性增加和TP53蛋白磷酸化增强。STRING分析显示,在HPV阳性样本中,JAK-STAT信号通路、白细胞介素-23信号通路和凋亡通路参与其中。在HPV阴性样本中,三尖杉酯碱靶点的富集与RUNX3调控CDKN1A转录以及白细胞介素-4、白细胞介素-13和白细胞介素-17信号通路相关。结论:三尖杉酯碱在HPV阳性HNSCC中表现出体外化学增敏作用,可能通过重新激活野生型TP53并恢复凋亡控制实现。炎症细胞因子信号通路的阻断可能为这种化学增敏作用提供了额外的机制。科学与公共卫生意义:研究结果表明,三尖杉酯碱可能是一种有前景的化学增敏剂,用于HPV阳性HNSCC的治疗,通过重新激活TP53并恢复凋亡通路,可能改善治疗治疗效果。这可能为未来针对HPV相关癌症的靶向治疗研究提供信息。局限性与证据强度:本研究仅限于体外分析,未包括临床试验或长期结果。由于基于实验室实验和计算分析,而没有直接的临床验证,证据强度中等。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)是全球第七常见的癌症,近年来其病因已发生转变。人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是HNSCC的关键危险因素,且其发病率自1980年以来持续上升。
设计与对象
该研究通过体外MTT实验评估了三萜类化合物三尖杉酯碱对人乳头瘤病毒阳性及阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌细胞增殖的影响。通过报告基因实验和Western blot分别检测了三尖杉酯碱对TP53启动子活性及TP53蛋白磷酸化的影响。
核心方法
通过体外MTT实验评估了三萜类化合物三尖杉酯碱对高危型人乳头瘤病毒阳性及阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌细胞增殖的影响。随后,利用cBioPortal和癌症基因组数据库(TCGA)分析了三尖杉酯碱的基因靶点富集情况,并进行了STRING分析。
主要结果
三尖杉酯碱与低剂量顺铂联合使用时可作为化疗增敏剂。这种效应在人乳头瘤病毒阳性细胞中最为显著。人乳头瘤病毒阳性细胞还表现出TP53启动子活性增加和TP53蛋白磷酸化增强。STRING分析显示,人乳头瘤病毒阳性样本中涉及JAK-STAT信号通路、白细胞介素-23信号通路和细胞凋亡通路。在人乳头瘤病毒阴性样本中,三尖杉酯碱富集的目标与RUNX3调控CDKN1A转录以及白细胞介素-4、白细胞介素-13和白细胞介素-17信号通路相关。
解释与创新
三尖杉酯碱在人乳头瘤病毒阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌中作为体外化疗增敏剂起作用,可能通过重新激活野生型TP53和恢复凋亡调控来实现。炎症细胞因子信号通路阻断可能提供了另一种机制,有助于这种化疗增敏效应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,三尖杉酯碱可能是一种有前景的化学增敏剂,用于HPV阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌,可能通过重新激活TP53和恢复凋亡途径来改善治疗结果。这可能为未来针对HPV相关癌症的靶向治疗研究提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外分析,不包括临床试验或长期结果。证据等级为中等,因为其基于实验室实验和计算分析,而没有直接的临床验证。

Triptolide as a Chemosensitizer in Human Papillomavirus Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma Through TP53.

Authors: WILLIAM KAMM, Q I Zhang, LINDSEY MORTENSEN, BEVERLY WUERTZ, FRANK G. ONDREY
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND/AIM: Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is the seventh most common cancer globally and has demonstrated an etiological shift over recent decades. Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a key risk factor for HNSCC, and its incidence has been rising since 1980. HPV-associated HNSCC display a unique tumorigenesis process and respond differently to traditional HNSCC treatment. Triptolide, a natural derivative, has shown promise as a chemosensitizer against HPV-positive HNSCC. The aim of this study was to investigate activity of triptolide in HNSCC in addition to in silico analysis of triptolide gene targets. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Effects of triptolide on proliferation of HPV-positive and HPV-negative HNSCC cells were evaluated using an MTT assay in vitro. Effects of triptolide on TP53 promoter activity and phosphorylation of TP53 protein were measured by reporter gene assay and western blot respectively. Next, enrichment of triptolide gene targets was evaluated through cBioPortal and The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) with a subsequent STRING analysis. RESULTS: Triptolide acts as a chemosensitizer in combination with low-dose cisplatin. This effect is most pronounced in HPV-positive cells. HPV-positive cells also show increased TP53 promoter activity and TP53 protein phosphorylation. STRING analysis shows involvement of JAK-STAT signaling, interleukin-23 signaling, and apoptosis pathways in HPV-positive samples. In HPV-negative samples, enriched triptolide targets are implicated in RUNX3 regulation of CDKN1A transcription and interleukin-4, interleukin-13, and interleukin-17 signaling. CONCLUSION: Triptolide acts as an in vitro chemosensitizer in HPV-positive HNSCC, likely through reactivation of wild-type TP53 and restoration of apoptotic control. Inflammatory cytokine signaling blockade may provide an additional mechanism contributing to this chemosensitizing effect.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is the seventh most common cancer globally and has demonstrated an etiological shift over recent decades. HPV infection is a key risk factor for HNSCC, and its incidence has been rising since 1980.
Design and population
The study evaluated the effects of triptolide on proliferation of HPV-positive and HPV-negative HNSCC cells using an MTT assay in vitro. Effects of triptolide on TP53 promoter activity and phosphorylation of TP53 protein were measured by reporter gene assay and western blot respectively.
Core methods
Effects of triptolide on proliferation of HPV-positive and HPV-negative HNSCC cells were evaluated using an MTT assay in vitro. Next, enrichment of triptolide gene targets was evaluated through cBioPortal and The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) with a subsequent STRING analysis.
Main results
Triptolide acts as a chemosensitizer in combination with low-dose cisplatin. This effect is most pronounced in HPV-positive cells. HPV-positive cells also show increased TP53 promoter activity and TP53 protein phosphorylation. STRING analysis shows involvement of JAK-STAT signaling, interleukin-23 signaling, and apoptosis pathways in HPV-positive samples. In HPV-negative samples, enriched triptolide targets are implicated in RUNX3 regulation of CDKN1A transcription and interleukin-4, interleukin-13, and interleukin-17 signaling.
Interpretation and novelty
Triptolide acts as an in vitro chemosensitizer in HPV-positive HNSCC, likely through reactivation of wild-type TP53 and restoration of apoptotic control. Inflammatory cytokine signaling blockade may provide an additional mechanism contributing to this chemosensitizing effect.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that triptolide may be a promising chemosensitizer for HPV-positive HNSCC, potentially improving treatment outcomes by reactivating TP53 and restoring apoptotic pathways. This could inform future research into targeted therapies for HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analysis and does not include clinical trials or long-term outcomes. The evidence is moderate as it is based on laboratory experiments and computational analysis without direct clinical validation.

HPV16基因组状态可区分口咽鳞状细胞癌疾病复发风险

HPV16 Genomic Status Stratifies Risk of Disease Recurrence in Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma.
作者： Victor Malassigné, Julien Puech, Sandrine Imbeaud, Anne Auperin, Caroline Even, Dac Hung Nguyen, Maxime Wack, Bastien Rance, Léa Benyakar, Léa Picavet
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:尽管预后总体良好,但 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 患者的一部分仍会出现疾病复发。仍然缺乏用于风险分层的可靠生物标志物。我们研究了全面的病毒基因组分析是否可以改善预后分类。实验设计:使用靶向病毒捕获测序对参加 2 个独立 队列研究 的 100 名 p16 阳性和 HPV DNA 阳性 OPSCC 患者的肿瘤样本进行分析。对病毒基因型、亚系、单核苷酸变异、病毒-宿主整合位点、病毒结构变异(SV)、克隆性和病毒拷贝数进行了表征。在 60 个月时评估与累积 发病率 进展、无进展生存期 (PFS) 和总生存期的关联。结果:94个肿瘤中检测到HPV16,其中83个属于HPV16_A1亚系。在该亚系中,20 个肿瘤具有严格附加型病毒 基因组,而 63 个则表现出整合和/或病毒 SV。在严格附加型 HPV16_A1 亚组中没有发生进展事件,而在具有整合和/或 SV 的 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中发生了 12 起进展事件。60 个月时,在严格附加型 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中没有观察到进展事件,而在具有整合和/或 SV 的 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中,累积进展 发病率 为 23.1%。先前报道的高风险单核苷酸变异和新型上游调控区变异与较差的 PFS 相关。整合位点在开放染色质和启动子区域富集,并聚集在病毒 E2 和 E4 基因内。结论:在 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中,病毒基因组结构定义了临床上不同的亚组。严格附加型(野生型)基因组 识别低风险人群,而整合和 SV 则表征高风险疾病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
摘要 目的:尽管总体预后较好,但一部分人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC)患者仍会发生疾病复发。目前仍缺乏可靠的生物标志物来进行风险分层。
设计与对象
我们调查了全面的病毒基因组分析是否能优化预后分类。来自100名p16阳性且HPV DNA阳性的OPSCC患者的肿瘤样本,在2个独立的队列研究研究中被分析,使用了靶向病毒捕获测序技术。
核心方法
分析了病毒基因型、亚型、单核苷酸变异、病毒-宿主整合位点、病毒结构变异(SV)、克隆性以及病毒拷贝数。评估了这些特征与60个月随访期间疾病进展累积概率、无进展生存期(PFS)和总生存期的关系。
主要结果
在94例检测到HPV16的肿瘤中,83例属于HPV16_A1亚型。在该亚型中,20例肿瘤仅含有严格的游离病毒基因组,而63例则表现出整合和/或病毒结构变异。在严格的游离HPV16_A1亚组中未观察到任何进展事件,而在存在整合和/或结构变异的HPV16_A1肿瘤中观察到12例进展事件。在60个月随访时,严格的游离HPV16_A1肿瘤未观察到进展事件,而存在整合和/或结构变异的HPV16_A1肿瘤的进展累积概率为23.1%。
解释与创新
在HPV16_A1肿瘤中,病毒基因组结构定义了临床不同的亚组。严格的游离(野生型)病毒基因组标识出低风险人群,而整合和结构变异则代表高风险疾病。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发更精确的HPV相关口咽癌风险分层工具。它们也可能指导基于病毒基因组状态的靶向治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于仅针对单一HPV类型和亚型,且证据基于特定的队列研究。由于可能存在泛化能力的局限性,证据强度中等。

HPV16 Genomic Status Stratifies Risk of Disease Recurrence in Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma.

Authors: Victor Malassigné, Julien Puech, Sandrine Imbeaud, Anne Auperin, Caroline Even, Dac Hung Nguyen, Maxime Wack, Bastien Rance, Léa Benyakar, Léa Picavet
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose: A subset of patients with human papillomavirus (HPV)–positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) develops disease recurrence despite generally favorable outcomes. Reliable biomarkers for risk stratification remain lacking. We investigated whether comprehensive viral genomic profiling refines prognostic classification. Experimental Design: Tumor samples from 100 patients with p16-positive and HPV DNA–positive OPSCC enrolled in 2 independent cohorts were analyzed using targeted viral capture sequencing. Viral genotype, sublineage, single-nucleotide variation, virus–host integration sites, viral structural variants (SV), clonality, and viral copy number were characterized. Associations with cumulative incidence of progression, progression-free survival (PFS), and overall survival were assessed at 60 months. Results: HPV16 was detected in 94 tumors, 83 of which belonged to the HPV16_A1 sublineage. Within this sublineage, 20 tumors harbored strictly episomal viral genomes, whereas 63 exhibited integration and/or viral SV. No progression events occurred in the strictly episomal HPV16_A1 subgroup, whereas 12 occurred among HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV. At 60 months, no progression events were observed in strictly episomal HPV16_A1 tumors compared with a 23.1% cumulative incidence of progression in HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV. Previously reported high-risk single-nucleotide variants and a novel upstream regulatory region variant were associated with worse PFS. Integration sites were enriched in open chromatin and promoter regions and clustered within viral E2 and E4 genes. Conclusions: Within HPV16_A1 tumors, viral genomic architecture defines clinically distinct subgroups. Strictly episomal (wild-type) genomes identify a low-risk population, whereas integration and SV characterize higher-risk disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Abstract Purpose: A subset of patients with human papillomavirus (HPV)–positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) develops disease recurrence despite generally favorable outcomes. Reliable biomarkers for risk stratification remain lacking.
Design and population
We investigated whether comprehensive viral genomic profiling refines prognostic classification. Tumor samples from 100 patients with p16-positive and HPV DNA–positive OPSCC enrolled in 2 independent cohorts were analyzed using targeted viral capture sequencing.
Core methods
Viral genotype, sublineage, single-nucleotide variation, virus–host integration sites, viral structural variants (SV), clonality, and viral copy number were characterized. Associations with cumulative incidence of progression, progression-free survival (PFS), and overall survival were assessed at 60 months.
Main results
HPV16 was detected in 94 tumors, 83 of which belonged to the HPV16_A1 sublineage. Within this sublineage, 20 tumors harbored strictly episomal viral genomes, whereas 63 exhibited integration and/or viral SV. No progression events occurred in the strictly episomal HPV16_A1 subgroup, whereas 12 occurred among HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV. At 60 months, no progression events were observed in strictly episomal HPV16_A1 tumors compared with a 23.1% cumulative incidence of progression in HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV.
Interpretation and novelty
Within HPV16_A1 tumors, viral genomic architecture defines clinically distinct subgroups. Strictly episomal (wild-type) genomes identify a low-risk population, whereas integration and SV characterize higher-risk disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of more precise risk stratification tools for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer. They could also guide targeted therapeutic strategies based on viral genomic status.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single HPV type and sublineage, and the evidence is based on a specific cohort. The evidence strength is moderate due to the potential for generalizability limitations.

荧光生物传感技术用于同时检测HPV16和HPV18。

Fluorescence biosensing for simultaneous detection of HPV16 and HPV18.
作者： Ze-Lin Wang, Zhao-Li Mou, Can Yang, Sheng-Liang Jin, Han-Ying Zhan, Zhi-Qi Zhang
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌、大多数肛门癌以及大多数口咽癌是由人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)引起的,其中HPV16和HPV18占大多数病例,因此需要同时检测多种HPV类型以识别HPV感染、进行风险分层和筛查HPV相关疾病。熵驱动的催化链置换反应是基于DNA自组装的信号放大方法,无需酶参与。此外,其驱动力源自系统内部,赋予其高特异性,这对于同时检测多个目标具有关键优势。因此,开发了一种分析方法,通过双脚手架介导的链置换反应放大荧光信号,从而实现对HPV18和HPV16的同时检测。对于这两种目标,该方法在浓度范围0.05至5 nM内表现出良好的回归曲线线性。HPV18和HPV16的检测限分别为0.020和0.033 nM,即使仅用于检测单一HPV类型,这些数值也极具优势。这些结果表明,所开发的无酶检测方法具有高灵敏度和特异性,使其成为快速且经济有效的HPV16和HPV18筛查的有前景技术。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种同时检测HPV16和HPV18的方法,因为它们在导致宫颈癌、肛门癌和口咽癌中起着重要作用,需要准确识别以进行风险分层和筛查。
设计与对象
本研究涉及开发一种用于检测HPV16和HPV18的分析方法,重点在于检测方法的技术方面,而非特定人群或环境。
核心方法
该方法利用了熵驱动的催化链置换反应,该反应依赖于DNA自组装进行信号放大,无需酶参与。该检测方法在HPV16和HPV18的浓度范围0.05至5 nM内表现出良好的线性。
主要结果
HPV18和HPV16的检测限分别为0.020和0.033 nM,表明该方法对这两种目标具有高灵敏度和特异性。
解释与创新
所开发的无酶检测方法为快速且经济有效的HPV16和HPV18筛查提供了一种有前景的技术,利用熵驱动反应的内在特异性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为HPV筛查方法提供了潜在的进展,可能有助于提高HPV相关癌症的早期检测和风险评估能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究未报告局限性,但该方法的高灵敏度和特异性表明其在HPV筛查中的应用具有强有力的证据。

Fluorescence biosensing for simultaneous detection of HPV16 and HPV18.

Authors: Ze-Lin Wang, Zhao-Li Mou, Can Yang, Sheng-Liang Jin, Han-Ying Zhan, Zhi-Qi Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Cervical, most anal, and most oropharyngeal cancers are caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), with HPV16 and HPV18 accounting for the majority of cases, necessitating simultaneous detection of multiple HPV types for identifying HPV infection, risk stratification, and screening for HPV-associated disease. Entropy-driven catalytic strand displacement reactions are DNA self-assembly amplification methods that do not require enzymes for signal amplification. Furthermore, their driving forces originate within the system, endowing them with high specificity, which provides a crucial advantage for simultaneous detection of several targets. Therefore, an analytical method was developed to amplify fluorescence signals via dual-toehold-mediated strand displacement, enabling simultaneous detection of HPV18 and HPV16. For both targets, the established method shows excellent linearity of the regression curve in the concentration range from 0.05 to 5 nM. The detection limits for HPV18 and HPV16 were 0.020 and 0.033 nM, respectively, which are highly favorable even for the detection of only a single HPV type. These results show that the developed enzyme-free assay has high sensitivity and specificity, making it a promising technique for rapid and cost-effective HPV16 and HPV18 screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for simultaneous detection of HPV16 and HPV18 due to their significant role in causing cervical, anal, and oropharyngeal cancers, which necessitates accurate identification for risk stratification and screening.
Design and population
The study involved the development of an analytical method for detecting HPV16 and HPV18, focusing on the technical aspects of the assay rather than specific populations or settings.
Core methods
The method utilized entropy-driven catalytic strand displacement reactions, which rely on DNA self-assembly for signal amplification without requiring enzymes. The assay demonstrated excellent linearity in the concentration range of 0.05 to 5 nM for both HPV16 and HPV18.
Main results
The detection limits for HPV18 and HPV16 were 0.020 and 0.033 nM, respectively, indicating high sensitivity and specificity for these targets.
Interpretation and novelty
The developed enzyme-free assay provides a promising technique for rapid and cost-effective screening of HPV16 and HPV18, leveraging the inherent specificity of entropy-driven reactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a potential advancement in HPV screening methods, which could improve early detection and risk assessment for HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the method's high sensitivity and specificity suggest strong evidence for its utility in HPV screening.

昆士兰澳大利亚原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民青少年及家长对通过昆士兰学校免疫计划进行人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)疫苗接种的看法:一项定性研究。

Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescent and parent perspectives on human papillomavirus vaccination through the School Immunisation Program in Queensland, Australia: a qualitative study.
作者： Tamara L. Butler, Kate Anderson, Ami Morseu-Diop, Julia M.L. Brotherton, Joan Cunningham, Allison Jaure, Gail Garvey, Evan AhWing, Vanessa Clements, Sonya Egert
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解影响昆士兰原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民青少年参与学校 HPV 疫苗接种 疫苗接种计划的障碍和促进因素,鉴于这些人群面临更高的 宫颈癌(宫颈癌)负担。
设计与对象
本研究采用原住民研究方法和健康促进的生态社会模型,对来自10所多样化昆士兰学校的65名原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民青少年及20名家长进行了Yarns(一种文化适当性的定性方法)访谈。
核心方法
采用基于编码本的主题分析方法,以探讨对HPV及其疫苗接种(疫苗接种)的理解、信息和资源、疫苗(疫苗接种)知情同意、疫苗接种(疫苗接种)体验以及改进建议。
主要结果
本研究确定了两个主要主题:导致疫苗接种(疫苗接种)诊所的因素,以及疫苗接种(疫苗接种)诊所当天的因素。青少年和家长支持HPV 疫苗接种(疫苗接种),但在知情同意表的完成和沟通方面面临挑战。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,采用灵活的知情同意流程、提供文化安全的教育材料以及在疫苗接种诊所中安排原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民支持人员,可以提高这些青少年的HPV疫苗接种率。
科研与公卫意义
这些建议可以指导计划的调整和干预措施,以提高原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民青少年的HPV疫苗接种率,从而推进在宫颈癌(宫颈癌)结果方面的公平性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括其定性性质以及样本中可能存在偏倚,但研究结果为提高这些人群的疫苗接种(疫苗接种)率提供了有价值的见解。

Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescent and parent perspectives on human papillomavirus vaccination through the School Immunisation Program in Queensland, Australia: a qualitative study.

Authors: Tamara L. Butler, Kate Anderson, Ami Morseu-Diop, Julia M.L. Brotherton, Joan Cunningham, Allison Jaure, Gail Garvey, Evan AhWing, Vanessa Clements, Sonya Egert
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander peoples experience a greater burden of cervical cancer than other Australians. Vaccination can prevent diseases caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), but uptake remains inequitable. We aimed to understand barriers and enablers influencing the participation of Queensland Aboriginalmed and Torres Strait Islander adolescents in the school-based HPV vaccination program. METHODS: Using an Indigenist research approach and guided by a socio-ecological model for health promotion, Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander voices were centred in this project. In 2020-2021 we conducted Yarns (a culturally appropriate qualitative method) with 65 Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents and 20 parents from 10 diverse Queensland schools. Yarns addressed understandings of HPV and HPV vaccination, information and resources, vaccine consent, experience of vaccination, and suggestions for improvement. Thematic analysis using a codebook approach was conducted. RESULTS: The first major theme related to factors leading up to the vaccination clinic. Despite varying levels of knowledge and understanding of HPV and HPV vaccination, most adolescents and parents supported the idea of vaccinations to prevent diseases and benefit their health. Adolescents and parents reported difficulties completing and returning the consent form and wanted more accessible information and improved communication about the vaccine, delivered in culturally appropriate ways. The second major theme related to factors on the day of the vaccination clinic. Adolescents reported a need for privacy during vaccination, ways to manage heightened emotions triggered by the vaccination clinic, and the presence of trusted support people. Findings were contextualised within the COVID-19 pandemic and vaccination rollout, with the pandemic causing disruptions to the school program and the HPV vaccine perceived as more trustworthy than the then-new COVID-19 vaccines. CONCLUSIONS: We provide several recommendations to support Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents to be vaccinated against HPV, such as flexible consent processes with assistance from Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander support staff; availability of culturally safe educational materials for adolescents, parents and school staff; the presence of Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander support staff at clinics, and adherence to optimal clinic set up and scheduling to improve adolescents' experience of vaccination. These recommendations can guide programmatic changes and interventions to increase HPV vaccination rates among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents, thereby advancing equity in cervical cancer outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand barriers and enablers influencing the participation of Queensland Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents in the school-based HPV vaccination program, given the greater burden of cervical cancer among these populations.
Design and population
The study used an Indigenist research approach and socio-ecological model for health promotion, involving Yarns (a culturally appropriate qualitative method) with 65 Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents and 20 parents from 10 diverse Queensland schools.
Core methods
Thematic analysis using a codebook approach was conducted to address understandings of HPV and HPV vaccination, information and resources, vaccine consent, experience of vaccination, and suggestions for improvement.
Main results
The study identified two major themes: factors leading up to the vaccination clinic and factors on the day of the vaccination clinic. Adolescents and parents supported HPV vaccination but faced challenges with consent forms and communication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that flexible consent processes, culturally safe educational materials, and the presence of Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander support staff can improve HPV vaccination rates among these adolescents.
Scientific and public-health significance
These recommendations can guide programmatic changes and interventions to increase HPV vaccination rates among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander adolescents, thereby advancing equity in cervical cancer outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its qualitative nature and the potential for bias in the sample, but the findings provide valuable insights into improving vaccination rates among these populations.

人乳头瘤病毒 外生殖器部位的尖锐湿疣病例,美国纽约,2025年。

Human Papillomavirus Condylomas of Extragenital Site in Immunocompetent Man, New York, USA, 2025.

Authors: Sabrina Fong, John G. Zampella
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus types 6 and 11 are associated with mucosal condylomata acuminata, with rare reports of extragenital manifestations. We report a case of condyloma localized to the neck of an immunocompetent heterosexual man, without concurrent anogenital lesions. Our findings challenge theories of the site-specific tropism of these human papillomavirus types.

患病率和高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)在南非高HIV负担农村地区青少年女孩和年轻女性中的分布:一项横断面调查

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) among adolescent girls and young women in a high HIV burden rural area of South Africa: a cross-sectional survey
作者： Maphe Mthembu, Kathy Baisley, Jacob Busang, Nqobile Ngoma, Nonsikelelo Ndlela, Nonhlanhla Okesola, Carina Herbst, Thandeka Khoza, Jaco Dreyer, Lenine Liebenberg
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV是宫颈癌的主要病因,HIV感染者女性感染HPV及其相关疾病的风险更高。
设计与对象
本横断面研究嵌套于Thetha Nami基于人群的试验基线调查中,调查对象为夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省农村uMkhanyakude地区15至30岁年轻人。我们选择了有可用阴道拭子的AGYW HIV感染者,并选取了与HIV感染频率匹配的AGYW非HIV感染者样本。
核心方法
使用HPV Direct Flow Chip Kit(Master Diagnostica,西班牙)进行HPV基因型检测。使用逻辑回归分析确定与HPV感染相关的因素。
主要结果
在187名具有有效HPV检测结果的AGYW中,90名(48.1%)为HIV感染者。总体HPV患病率为69.1%(95%置信区间(CI)= 54.5–80.7%),高危型(hr)HPV患病率为57.3%(95%CI = 44.1–69.6%)。HIV感染者AGYW的HPV患病率高于非HIV感染者AGYW(84.6% vs 64.6%,p = 0.02)。HPV基因型分布因HIV状态而异:HIV感染者AGYW中最常见的高危型HPV基因型为HPV35、HPV45和HPV58,而非HIV感染者AGYW中最常见的为HPV52、HPV56和HPV31。在年龄符合国家HPV 疫苗接种计划的参与者中,未检测到HPV16或HPV18感染。
解释与创新
非十价疫苗疫苗覆盖的非HPV16/18高危型HPV基因型的患病率较高,突显了其更广谱基因型覆盖的潜在价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在高HIV负担地区扩大HPV筛查和宫颈癌预防计划。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) among adolescent girls and young women in a high HIV burden rural area of South Africa: a cross-sectional survey

Authors: Maphe Mthembu, Kathy Baisley, Jacob Busang, Nqobile Ngoma, Nonsikelelo Ndlela, Nonhlanhla Okesola, Carina Herbst, Thandeka Khoza, Jaco Dreyer, Lenine Liebenberg
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the primary cause of cervical cancer, with women living with HIV at increased risk of HPV infection and HPV-related disease. Data on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution among adolescent girls and young women (AGYW) in rural South African settings remain limited. We estimated HPV prevalence, genotype distribution, and factors associated with HPV infection among AGYW in a high HIV prevalence district of KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa. Methods This cross-sectional study was nested within the baseline survey of the Thetha Nami population-based trial among young people aged 15–30 years in rural uMkhanyakude District, KwaZulu-Natal. Survey participants were asked to provide dried blood spots for HIV testing and genital swabs for STI testing. For this study, we selected AGYW living with HIV with available vaginal swabs and a frequency-matched sample of AGYW without HIV. HPV genotyping was done using the HPV Direct Flow Chip Kit (Master Diagnostica, Spain). Genotype-specific HPV prevalence was described overall and by HIV status. Logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with HPV infection. Results Among 187 AGYW with valid HPV results, 90 (48.1%) were living with HIV. Overall HPV prevalence was 69.1% (95% confidence interval (CI) = 54.5–80.7%) and high-risk (hr)HPV prevalence was 57.3% (95%CI = 44.1–69.6%). HPV prevalence was higher among AGYW living with HIV than among AGYW without HIV (84.6% vs 64.6%, p = 0.02). HPV genotype distribution varied by HIV status: HPV35, HPV45 and HPV58 were the most prevalent hrHPV genotypes among AGYW living with HIV, vs HPV52, HPV56 and HPV31 among AGYW without HIV. No HPV16 or HPV18 infections were detected among participants who were age-eligible for the national HPV vaccination programme. Reported condomless sex (adjusted odds ratio (1) 2.23, 95%CI = 1.06–4.70) and the presence of another sexually transmitted infection (aOR = 4.30, 95%CI = 1.52–12.20) were associated with HPV infection. Conclusions AGYW in rural uMkhanyakude experience a high burden of hrHPV infection, especially those living with HIV. Regardless of HPV vaccination status not being confirmed, no HPV 16/18 was detected among vaccine-eligible AGYW. The higher prevalence of non-HPV16/18 hrHPV genotypes covered by the nonavalent vaccine highlights the potential value of vaccines with broader genotype coverage. These findings support expanding HPV screening and cervical cancer prevention programmes in high HIV prevalence settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV is the primary cause of cervical cancer, with women living with HIV at increased risk of HPV infection and HPV-related disease.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was nested within the baseline survey of the Thetha Nami population-based trial among young people aged 15–30 years in rural uMkhanyakude District, KwaZulu-Natal. We selected AGYW living with HIV with available vaginal swabs and a frequency-matched sample of AGYW without HIV.
Core methods
HPV genotyping was done using the HPV Direct Flow Chip Kit (Master Diagnostica, Spain). Logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with HPV infection.
Main results
Among 187 AGYW with valid HPV results, 90 (48.1%) were living with HIV. Overall HPV prevalence was 69.1% (95% confidence interval (CI) = 54.5–80.7%) and high-risk (hr)HPV prevalence was 57.3% (95%CI = 44.1–69.6%). HPV prevalence was higher among AGYW living with HIV than among AGYW without HIV (84.6% vs 64.6%, p = 0.02). HPV genotype distribution varied by HIV status: HPV35, HPV45 and HPV58 were the most prevalent hrHPV genotypes among AGYW living with HIV, vs HPV52, HPV56 and HPV31 among AGYW without HIV. No HPV16 or HPV18 infections were detected among participants who were age-eligible for the national HPV vaccination programme.
Interpretation and novelty
The higher prevalence of non-HPV16/18 hrHPV genotypes covered by the nonavalent vaccine highlights the potential value of vaccines with broader genotype coverage.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support expanding HPV screening and cervical cancer prevention programmes in high HIV prevalence settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

健康素养如何影响人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)预防性医疗决策?

How Does Health Literacy Shape Preventive Healthcare Decisions on Human Papillomavirus Vaccination?
作者： Vasundhara Yadav, Ananta Ganjoo
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一种针对持续高危型HPV感染的预防性医疗干预措施,该感染会导致宫颈癌及其他HPV相关癌症。尽管已有有效的HPV疫苗,并且在许多国家已将HPV疫苗纳入国家预防战略,但疫苗接种率仍取决于除可获得性之外的其他因素。因此,理解健康素养对HPV疫苗预防性医疗决策的影响非常重要。已有研究表明,健康素养与HPV疫苗之间的关系因群体、年龄和疫苗类型而异,而关于疫苗相关健康素养的新兴研究则表明,这种关系并非单向。在大学生群体中,有关HPV特异性健康素养的证据更为充分,显示出健康素养与HPV知识之间存在正向关联,且接种疫苗的学生与未接种的学生在知识得分上存在差异。研究还指出,健康素养问题既是大学生HPV疫苗决策的促进因素,也是阻碍因素。基于现有证据,本文认为健康素养应被视为HPV疫苗项目的重要组成部分,但不应被视为解决疫苗犹豫的万能钥匙。健康素养影响HPV疫苗决策,是更广泛医疗体系的一部分,与其他因素如医生建议、社会影响、经济负担、可及性、基于性别态度、文化、错误信息及其他医疗体系障碍共同作用。本文还建议采用一种关注健康素养的框架,包括可访问的沟通方式、可信赖的信息、医生参与、文化敏感的信息、数字健康素养、HPV疫苗决策支持以及便捷的HPV疫苗获取途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨健康素养如何影响与HPV疫苗相关的预防性医疗决策,因为HPV疫苗是针对持续高危型HPV感染的关键预防性医疗干预措施,该感染会导致宫颈癌及其他HPV相关癌症。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于理解健康素养对HPV疫苗决策的影响,特别是针对大学生群体,因为在此群体中有关HPV特异性健康素养的证据更为充分,并显示出健康素养与HPV知识之间的正向关联。
核心方法
本研究通过分析接种疫苗和未接种疫苗学生的知识得分,探讨健康素养与HPV疫苗决策之间的关系,识别健康素养问题作为HPV疫苗决策的促进因素和阻碍因素。
主要结果
本研究发现,健康素养与HPV知识相关,接种疫苗和未接种疫苗学生之间的知识得分存在差异,且健康素养问题被识别为大学生HPV疫苗决策的促进因素和阻碍因素。
解释与创新
作者认为,健康素养影响HPV疫苗决策,是更广泛医疗体系的一部分,与其他因素如医生建议、社会影响、经济负担、可及性、基于性别态度、文化、错误信息及其他医疗体系障碍共同作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,健康素养应被视为HPV疫苗项目的重要组成部分,但不应是解决疫苗犹豫的唯一解决方案,强调了建立关注健康素养的框架以改善HPV疫苗结果的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要内容,虽然提供了关于健康素养在HPV疫苗决策中作用的见解,但并未提供详细的统计分析或更广泛的人群数据,这限制了证据的强度。

How Does Health Literacy Shape Preventive Healthcare Decisions on Human Papillomavirus Vaccination?

Authors: Vasundhara Yadav, Ananta Ganjoo
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a preventive health intervention for persistent high-risk HPV infection that causes cervical and other HPV-associated cancers. Despite the availability of effective HPV vaccines and the integration of vaccination into national preventive strategies in many countries, uptake depends on factors beyond availability. Therefore, it is important to understand the influence of health literacy on preventive healthcare decisions about HPV vaccination. It is established that the link between health literacy and vaccination varies by group, age, and vaccine, and emerging work on vaccine literacy indicates that this link is not unidirectional. Evidence on HPV-specific health literacy is stronger among university students, showing a positive association between health literacy and knowledge of HPV, with knowledge scores varying between vaccinated and unvaccinated students, and health-literacy issues have been identified as both enablers of and barriers to college students’ vaccination decision-making. Based on the evidence, the paper argues that health literacy should be considered an integral part of HPV vaccination programmes, but not the cure-all for vaccine hesitancy. Health literacy affects vaccination as part of a wider health system alongside other factors such as provider recommendation, social influence, affordability, access, gender-based attitudes, culture, misinformation, and other health-system barriers. The paper also recommends a health-literacy–sensitive framework of accessible communication, trustworthy information, provider engagement, culturally sensitive information, digital health literacy, vaccination decision support, and easy vaccination access.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how health literacy influences preventive healthcare decisions regarding HPV vaccination, as HPV vaccination is a critical preventive health intervention for persistent high-risk HPV infection that leads to cervical and other HPV-associated cancers.
Design and population
The study focused on understanding the impact of health literacy on HPV vaccination decisions, particularly among university students, as evidence on HPV-specific health literacy is stronger in this group and shows a positive association between health literacy and knowledge of HPV.
Core methods
The study examined the relationship between health literacy and vaccination decision-making by analyzing knowledge scores among vaccinated and unvaccinated students, identifying health-literacy issues as both enablers and barriers to vaccination decisions.
Main results
The study found that health literacy is associated with knowledge of HPV, with knowledge scores varying between vaccinated and unvaccinated students, and that health-literacy issues have been identified as both enablers of and barriers to college students’ vaccination decision-making.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that health literacy affects vaccination as part of a wider health system, alongside other factors such as provider recommendation, social influence, affordability, access, gender-based attitudes, culture, misinformation, and other health-system barriers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that health literacy should be considered an integral part of HPV vaccination programs, but not the sole solution for vaccine hesitancy, highlighting the need for a health-literacy–sensitive framework to improve vaccination outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and while it provides insights into the role of health literacy in HPV vaccination decisions, it does not offer detailed statistical analyses or broader population data, limiting the strength of the evidence.

人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)和宫颈癌知识在沙特女性中的社会人口学相关性

Sociodemographic correlates of human papilloma virus (HPV) and cervical cancer knowledge among Saudi women
作者： Yousif A. Kariri, Ibrahim A. Kuriry, Hassan A. Albargy, Fatmah A. Keraryi, Mansour A. Alsaleem
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是全球最常见的一种性传播病毒之一,具有严重的临床后果,例如宫颈癌。本研究旨在评估沙特女性对HPV和疫苗的知识、意识、态度和信念。研究于2024年12月9日至2025年5月9日间在沙特阿拉伯多个地区进行,采用横断面描述性研究设计,通过在线调查收集数据,包括女性的人口学特征、对HPV和疫苗接种的知识、意识、态度和信念。共有627名女性参与了本研究。大多数参与者(32.1%)年龄在36至45岁之间。总体而言,47.4%的参与者对HPV有充分的知识,51.5%知道其为性传播病毒,41.3%知道其可导致宫颈癌。主要的信息来源是卫生部和社会媒体。约66.7%的参与者认为HPV 疫苗不安全。婚姻状况、宗教信仰和教育水平与HPV的整体知识和意识以及对疫苗的认知显著相关。年龄、婚姻状况、子女数量、地区、教育水平和职业与对HPV的态度信念显著相关。本研究指出在HPV和宫颈癌的知识和意识方面存在差距,发现对疫苗接种并发症的风险担忧是接受疫苗的障碍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估沙特女性对HPV和疫苗的知识和意识,以解决对HPV和宫颈癌公众认知和信息来源理解方面的知识缺口。
设计与对象
本研究于2024年12月9日至2025年5月9日间在沙特阿拉伯女性中进行,涉及627名参与者。
核心方法
数据通过在线调查收集,包括人口学特征、对HPV和疫苗接种的知识、意识、态度和信念。
主要结果
47.4%的参与者对HPV有充分了解,51.5%知道它是通过性传播的,而41.3%知道它可能导致宫颈癌。婚姻状况、宗教信仰和教育水平与知识和意识相关,而年龄、婚姻状况、子女、地区、教育程度和职业与信念相关。
解释与创新
本研究突出了在HPV和宫颈癌的知识和意识方面存在的差距,指出对疫苗的安全性担忧是其接受率的障碍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为改善HPV和疫苗的教育和意识提供公共卫生策略,特别是针对特定的人口统计学群体。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计、依赖自我报告数据以及可能存在的社会期望偏差,这可能影响研究结果的普遍适用性。

Sociodemographic correlates of human papilloma virus (HPV) and cervical cancer knowledge among Saudi women

Authors: Yousif A. Kariri, Ibrahim A. Kuriry, Hassan A. Albargy, Fatmah A. Keraryi, Mansour A. Alsaleem
Original abstract (English)
Human papilloma virus (HPV) is one of the most frequent sexually transmitted viruses globally, with serious clinical consequences, such as cervical cancer. This study aims to evaluate knowledge and awareness of, and attitudes and beliefs towards, HPV and vaccines for it among women from several Saudi Arabian regions. A cross-sectional descriptive study was carried out among Saudi Arabian women. Data were collected from December 9, 2024, to May 9, 2025, via an online survey including women’s demographics, knowledge, awareness, attitudes, and beliefs regarding HPV and vaccination against it. An overall of 627 women took part in the study. The majority of the population (32.1%) is aged from 36 to 45 years. In total, 47.4% of the participants have an adequate level of knowledge of HPV, 51.5% know that it is sexually transmitted, and 41.3% know that it can cause cervical cancer. The main information sources were the Ministry of Health and social media. Around 66.7% of the participants believed that the HPV vaccine is not safe. Marital status, religion, and educational status were demonstrably associated with overall knowledge and awareness of HPV and a vaccine for it. Age, marital status, children, region, education level, and occupation were significantly associated with beliefs concerning HPV. This study reported a gap in awareness and knowledge of cervical cancer and HPV; risk and HPV vaccination complications were found to be barriers to vaccine acceptance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate knowledge and awareness of HPV and vaccines among Saudi women, addressing a knowledge gap in understanding public perception and information sources regarding HPV and cervical cancer.
Design and population
A cross-sectional descriptive study was conducted among Saudi Arabian women from December 9, 2024, to May 9, 2025, involving 627 participants.
Core methods
Data were collected via an online survey including demographics, knowledge, awareness, attitudes, and beliefs regarding HPV and vaccination.
Main results
47.4% of participants had adequate knowledge of HPV, 51.5% knew it was sexually transmitted, and 41.3% knew it could cause cervical cancer. Marital status, religion, and education were associated with knowledge and awareness, while age, marital status, children, region, education level, and occupation were associated with beliefs.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted a gap in awareness and knowledge of HPV and cervical cancer, with safety concerns about the vaccine being a barrier to acceptance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies to improve education and awareness about HPV and vaccines, particularly targeting specific demographic groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, reliance on self-reported data, and potential for social desirability bias, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

HPV 疫苗接种 对英格兰2016-2025年接受宫颈筛查的25-29岁女性中 高危型HPV 患病率 的影响

Impact of HPV vaccination on high-risk HPV prevalence in 25-29-year-old women attending cervical screening in England 2016-2025
作者： Lucinda Slater, Kavita Panwar, Marta Checchi, Laura Viviani, Alexandra Sargent, Viki Frew, Bethan Mackenzie, Simon A. Beddows, Kate Soldan
摘要中文翻译背景:在英格兰,一项基于学校的全国性 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种 计划于2008年9月启动。该计划为12-13岁的女孩提供常规 疫苗接种 针对HPV16/18,并在计划的前两年为14-18岁的女孩提供‘补种’机会。方法:收集了2016年至2025年间英格兰接受宫颈筛查的19,407名25-29岁女性的数据,并对其中4,837名高危型(HR)HPV筛查阳性的女性的残留样本进行基因分型,以监测特定高危型HPV的患病率并评估疫苗接种计划的影响。结果:共有3,683名HR-HPV筛查阳性的样本获得了特定高危型HPV的检测结果。与疫苗接种不符合条件的女性相比,常规符合条件的女性中HPV16和HPV18的年龄调整后患病率分别降低了91%(95%置信区间[CI]: 87-94%)和84%(95%CI: 65-93%)。此外,HPV31和HPV45也观察到显著下降(分别为85% [95%CI: 72-92%] 和84% [95%CI: 73-90%]),但其他高危型HPV未见明显下降。在618名有细胞学异常的女性中,HPV16/18分别在疫苗接种不符合条件的女性(53.9% [95%CI: 34.8-73.1%])、补种符合条件的女性(18.1% [95%CI: 13.5-22.7%])和常规符合条件的女性(6.5% [95%CI: 3.6-9.3%])的样本中被检测到。结论:英格兰HPV 疫苗接种 计划的早期阶段对HPV16、18、31和45提供了显著的保护作用,并极大地改变了宫颈疾病筛查策略的目标剩余。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估HPV 疫苗接种 计划对英格兰接受宫颈筛查的25-29岁女性中 高危型HPV 患病率 的影响。
设计与对象
对2016年至2025年间英格兰接受宫颈筛查的19,407名25-29岁女性的数据进行了回顾性分析,并对其中4,837名高危型(HR)HPV筛查阳性的女性的残留样本进行了基因分型。
核心方法
对高危型HPV筛查阳性的女性的残留样本进行基因分型,以监测特定高危型HPV的患病率并评估疫苗接种计划的影响。
主要结果
与疫苗接种不符合条件的女性相比,常规符合条件的女性中HPV16和HPV18的年龄调整后患病率分别降低了91%和84%。此外,HPV31和HPV45也观察到显著下降,但其他高危型HPV未见明显下降。在有细胞学异常的女性中,HPV16/18分别在疫苗接种不符合条件的女性(53.9% [95%CI: 34.8-73.1%])、补种符合条件的女性(18.1% [95%CI: 13.5-22.7%])和常规符合条件的女性(6.5% [95%CI: 3.6-9.3%])的样本中被检测到。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,英格兰HPV 疫苗接种 计划的早期阶段显著降低了特定高危型HPV类型的患病率,从而改变了宫颈疾病筛查策略的目标剩余。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为评估HPV 疫苗接种 在降低高危型HPV 患病率 方面的有效性提供了关键见解,有助于制定未来的宫颈癌筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于残留样本,未包含详细的统计分析,这可能限制了证据的强度。然而,观察到的特定HPV类型下降表明疫苗接种计划具有显著影响。

Impact of HPV vaccination on high-risk HPV prevalence in 25-29-year-old women attending cervical screening in England 2016-2025

Authors: Lucinda Slater, Kavita Panwar, Marta Checchi, Laura Viviani, Alexandra Sargent, Viki Frew, Bethan Mackenzie, Simon A. Beddows, Kate Soldan
Original abstract (English)
Background: In England, a national school-based human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination programme was introduced in September 2008. Routine vaccination against HPV16/18 is offered to girls aged 12-13 and a 'catch-up' offer ran for girls aged 14-18 during the programme's first two years. Methods: Data from 19,407 women aged 25-29yrs attending cervical screening across England 2016-2025 were collected and residual samples from 4,837 high-risk (HR) HPV-screening-positive women were genotyped to monitor type-specific HR-HPV prevalence and evaluate vaccination impact. Results: Type-specific HR-HPV results were available for 3,683 HR-HPV-screen-positive samples. Estimated age-adjusted prevalence of HPV16 and HPV18 was 91% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 87-94%) and 84% (95%CI: 65-93%) lower respectively among routine-eligible women than vaccination-ineligible women. Large declines were also observed for HPV31 (85% [95%CI: 72-92%]) and HPV45 (84% [95%CI: 73-90%]) but not for other HR-HPV types. Among 618 women with cytological abnormalities, HPV16/18 was present in samples from 53.9% (95%CI: 34.8-73.1%) of vaccination-ineligible, 18.1% (95%CI: 13.5-22.7%) of catch-up-eligible, and 6.5% (95%CI: 3.6-9.3%) of routine-eligible women. Conclusions: The early years of the HPV vaccination programme in England conferred strong protection against HPV16, 18, 31 and 45, and dramatically changed the target remaining for screening strategies to detect and reduce cervical disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of the HPV vaccination programme on high-risk HPV prevalence in 25-29-year-old women attending cervical screening in England.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of data from 19,407 women aged 25-29 years attending cervical screening across England between 2016 and 2025 was conducted, with residual samples from 4,837 HR-HPV-screening-positive women genotyped.
Core methods
Residual samples from HR-HPV-screening-positive women were genotyped to monitor type-specific HR-HPV prevalence and assess the vaccination programme's impact.
Main results
The age-adjusted prevalence of HPV16 and HPV18 was 91% and 84% lower, respectively, among routine-eligible women compared to vaccination-ineligible women. Large declines were also observed for HPV31 and HPV45, but not for other HR-HPV types. Among women with cytological abnormalities, HPV16/18 was present in 53.9%, 18.1%, and 6.5% of vaccination-ineligible, catch-up-eligible, and routine-eligible women, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the early years of the HPV vaccination programme in England significantly reduced the prevalence of specific high-risk HPV types, altering the target remaining for screening strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical insights into the effectiveness of HPV vaccination in reducing high-risk HPV prevalence, which can inform future cervical cancer screening and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on residual samples and does not include detailed statistical analyses, which may limit the strength of the evidence. However, the observed declines in specific HPV types suggest a strong impact of the vaccination programme.

VaIN和阴道癌患者中人乳头瘤病毒感染:一项回顾性横断面研究。

Human Papillomavirus Infection in Patients With VaIN and Vaginal Cancer: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study.
作者： Chenyao Guo, Zhongyue Yan, Jing Li, Yao Liu, Quanlong Lu, Ruifang Li, Chen Wang, Xinyi Zhao, Ruimei Feng, Zhilian Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染在阴道上皮内瘤变(VaIN)和阴道癌患者中的特征,以满足对阴道癌进行全面研究的需求。
设计与对象
本研究纳入了山西医科大学第二医院2014年1月至2023年12月期间因异常细胞学结果和/或阳性HPV检测而接受醋酸试验和活检的患者。
核心方法
根据组织学结果,选择患有良性阴道炎症反应、VaIN和阴道癌的患者作为研究对象。收集了年龄、绝经状态、临床表现和既往筛查史等临床数据。
主要结果
A total of 5,180 patients were included, with 2,739 having VaIN1, 641 having VaIN2/3, and 251 having vaginal cancer. HPV was detected in 4,548 patients, with the highest HPV positive rates in VaIN2/3 (95.5%), vaginal squamous cell carcinoma (VaSCC) (86.0%), and the lowest in adenocarcinoma of the vagina (43.8%). The most common HPV types were HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 18, with HPV18 being the highest in adenocarcinoma of the vagina.
解释与创新
HPV16、52、58、53和18是山西地区VaIN和阴道癌的主要基因型,HPV检测在宫颈癌筛查过程中对阴道病变的发现具有额外的益处。
科研与公卫意义
积极进行HPV 疫苗接种可能有助于减轻这些病变的负担,突显了HPV检测和疫苗接种在阴道癌和VaIN管理中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受到回顾性设计和单一中心特定人群纳入的限制,这可能影响其结果的普遍适用性。VaIN2/3和阴道癌中较高的HPV阳性率强调了进一步研究的必要性。

Human Papillomavirus Infection in Patients With VaIN and Vaginal Cancer: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study.

Authors: Chenyao Guo, Zhongyue Yan, Jing Li, Yao Liu, Quanlong Lu, Ruifang Li, Chen Wang, Xinyi Zhao, Ruimei Feng, Zhilian Wang
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Vaginal cancer is a rare malignancy, and the research on it is not comprehensive yet. To explore the characteristics of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in patients with vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN) and vaginal cancer. METHODS: This study included patients who underwent colposcopy and biopsy of suspected sites at the Second Hospital of Shanxi Medical University from January 2014 to December 2023 due to abnormal cytology results and/or positive HPV test. Those diagnosed with benign inflammatory reaction of the vagina (ie, vaginitis), VaIN and vaginal cancer according to histologic results were selected as the study objects. Clinical data such as age, menopause, clinical manifestations and previous screening history were collected. We aimed to analyze the distribution and characteristics of HPV in VaIN and vaginal cancer patients. RESULTS: A total of 5,180 patients were included in this study. Of the total, 2,739 patients had VaIN1, 641 had VaIN2/3, and 251 had vaginal cancer. There were 235 patients with vaginal squamous cell carcinoma (VaSCC) and 16 patients with vaginal adenocarcinoma. A total of 4,548 patients were infected with HPV. The HPV positive rate was 95.5% in VaIN2/3, 86.0% in VaSCC and 43.8% in adenocarcinoma of the vagina. The most common 5 HPV types were HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 18. The positive rate of HPV18 in the adenocarcinoma of the vagina was the highest. CONCLUSION: HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 18 are the predominant genotypes in VaIN and vaginal cancer in Shanxi, China. HPV testing provides additional benefits for the detection of vaginal lesions during cervical cancer screening. Active HPV vaccination may help reduce the burden of these lesions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the characteristics of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in patients with vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN) and vaginal cancer, addressing the need for comprehensive research on vaginal cancer.
Design and population
The study included patients from the Second Hospital of Shanxi Medical University who underwent colposcopy and biopsy due to abnormal cytology results and/or positive HPV test from January 2014 to December 2023.
Core methods
Patients with benign inflammatory reaction of the vagina, VaIN, and vaginal cancer were selected based on histologic results. Clinical data such as age, menopause, clinical manifestations, and previous screening history were collected.
Main results
A total of 5,180 patients were included, with 2,739 having VaIN1, 641 having VaIN2/3, and 251 having vaginal cancer. HPV was detected in 4,548 patients, with the highest HPV positive rates in VaIN2/3 (95.5%), vaginal squamous cell carcinoma (VaSCC) (86.0%), and the lowest in adenocarcinoma of the vagina (43.8%). The most common HPV types were HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 18, with HPV18 being the highest in adenocarcinoma of the vagina.
Interpretation and novelty
HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 18 are the predominant genotypes in VaIN and vaginal cancer in Shanxi, China, and HPV testing provides additional benefits for the detection of vaginal lesions during cervical cancer screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
Active HPV vaccination may help reduce the burden of these lesions, highlighting the importance of HPV testing and vaccination in the management of vaginal cancer and VaIN.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the inclusion of a specific population from a single center, which may affect generalizability. The high HPV positive rate in VaIN2/3 and vaginal cancer underscores the need for further research.

色谱LAMP与PCR在高危型HPV检测中的比较:对宫颈癌筛查的启示。

Comparison of Colorimetric LAMP and PCR for High-risk HPV Detection: Implications for Cervical Cancer Screening.
作者： Hee Yeun Yoon, Hyo Sung Jeon, Min Ju Kim, Jong Mi Kim, Yoo Young Lee, Gun Oh Chong
摘要中文翻译目的:宫颈癌主要由人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)感染引起,通常使用基于聚合酶链反应(PCR)的检测方法进行检测。然而,这些方法耗时且需要专门的仪器。本研究评估了环介导等温扩增(LAMP)检测方法作为快速替代方案在HPV检测中的诊断性能,并与传统PCR检测方法进行比较。方法:本研究纳入了933名≥19岁的非妊娠女性,她们接受了宫颈癌筛查或随访的HPV检测。HPV检测面板包括14种高危型(16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66和68型)。宫颈拭子样本经过核酸提取后,使用型特异性引物进行LAMP扩增。反应在63 °C下进行35分钟,结果通过颜色变化进行解读(蓝色表示阳性,紫色表示阴性)。结果:与PCR相比,LAMP检测方法对HPV 16和18型的敏感性为97.8%(95% CI:94.4-100.0),对其他高危型的敏感性为100.0%(95% CI:98.8-100.0)。所有类型的特异性为100.0%(95% CI:98.8-100.0)。总体诊断准确度为99.5%(95% CI:99.0-100.0)。当对所有14种HPV类型进行整体分析时,临床敏感性和特异性分别为99.4%和100.0%。结论:LAMP检测方法为HPV检测提供了一种快速、简便且准确的方法,可能在资源有限的环境中作为有用的床边筛查工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 主要由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起,通常使用基于聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 的检测进行检测。然而,这些方法非常耗时并且需要专门的仪器。
设计与对象
本研究评估了环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 检测作为 HPV 检测的快速替代方法与传统 PCR 检测相比的诊断性能。这项研究纳入了 933 名年龄≥19 岁的非孕妇,她们接受了 HPV 检测以进行 宫颈癌 筛查或随访。
核心方法
HPV 样本包括 14 种高危类型(16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68)。宫颈拭子样本经过核酸提取,然后使用类型特异性引物进行 LAMP 扩增。反应在 63 °C 下进行 35 分钟,并通过比色法解释结果(蓝色表示阳性,紫色表示阴性)。
主要结果
与PCR相比,LAMP检测方法对HPV 16和18型的敏感性为97.8%(95% CI:94.4-100.0),对其他高危型的敏感性为100.0%(95% CI:98.8-100.0)。所有类型的特异性为100.0%(95% CI:98.8-100.0)。总体诊断准确度为99.5%(95% CI:99.0-100.0)。
解释与创新
LAMP 检测提供了一种快速、简单且准确的 HPV 检测方法,可作为有用的护理点筛查工具,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LAMP可能成为宫颈癌筛查的宝贵工具,特别是在无法轻易获得基于PCR的方法的环境中。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于摘要分析,其证据基于LAMP和PCR方法的单一比较。由于缺乏关于长期结果或更广泛人群适用性的详细数据,证据强度中等。

Comparison of Colorimetric LAMP and PCR for High-risk HPV Detection: Implications for Cervical Cancer Screening.

Authors: Hee Yeun Yoon, Hyo Sung Jeon, Min Ju Kim, Jong Mi Kim, Yoo Young Lee, Gun Oh Chong
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Cervical cancer, primarily caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, is commonly detected using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based assays. However, these methods are time-consuming and require specialized instrumentation. This study evaluated the diagnostic performance of the loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assay as a rapid alternative for HPV detection compared with conventional PCR testing. METHODS: This study included 933 nonpregnant women aged ≥19 years who underwent HPV testing for cervical cancer screening or follow-up. The HPV panel included 14 high-risk types (16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68). Cervical swab samples underwent nucleic acid extraction followed by LAMP amplification using type-specific primers. Reactions were performed at 63 °C for 35 minutes, and results were interpreted colorimetrically (blue for positive, purple for negative). RESULTS: Compared with PCR, the LAMP assay showed a sensitivity of 97.8% (95% CI: 94.4-100.0) for HPV types 16 and 18 and 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8-100.0) for the remaining high-risk types. Specificity across all types was 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8-100.0). The overall diagnostic accuracy was 99.5% (95% CI: 99.0-100.0). When all 14 HPV types were analyzed collectively, clinical sensitivity and specificity were 99.4% and 100.0%, respectively. CONCLUSIONS: The LAMP assay provides a rapid, simple, and accurate method for HPV detection and may serve as a useful point-of-care screening tool, particularly in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer, primarily caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, is commonly detected using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based assays. However, these methods are time-consuming and require specialized instrumentation.
Design and population
This study evaluated the diagnostic performance of the loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assay as a rapid alternative for HPV detection compared with conventional PCR testing. This study included 933 nonpregnant women aged ≥19 years who underwent HPV testing for cervical cancer screening or follow-up.
Core methods
The HPV panel included 14 high-risk types (16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68). Cervical swab samples underwent nucleic acid extraction followed by LAMP amplification using type-specific primers. Reactions were performed at 63 °C for 35 minutes, and results were interpreted colorimetrically (blue for positive, purple for negative).
Main results
Compared with PCR, the LAMP assay showed a sensitivity of 97.8% (95% CI: 94.4-100.0) for HPV types 16 and 18 and 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8-100.0) for the remaining high-risk types. Specificity across all types was 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8-100.0). The overall diagnostic accuracy was 99.5% (95% CI: 99.0-100.0).
Interpretation and novelty
The LAMP assay provides a rapid, simple, and accurate method for HPV detection and may serve as a useful point-of-care screening tool, particularly in resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that LAMP could be a valuable tool for cervical cancer screening, especially in settings where PCR-based methods are not readily available.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence is based on a single comparison of LAMP and PCR methods. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed data on long-term outcomes or broader population applicability.

台湾大学生HPV预防健康素养量表的开发与心理测量学验证

Development and Psychometric Validation of the Human Papillomavirus Prevention Health Literacy Scale for College Students in Taiwan.
作者： Ching-Yi Lai, Shu-Chen Yang, Chieh-Yu Liu, Hung-Ru Lin, Yuan-Mei Liao, Tzu-Ying Lee
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一种常见的性传播病毒,与多种癌症有关。由于大学生属于高风险群体,了解他们关于HPV预防的健康素养至关重要。然而,目前尚无适合的测量工具可用于此目的。目的:本研究开发了HPV预防健康素养量表(HPV-HLS),并对其信度和效度进行了评估。方法:采用横断面研究设计,该量表在台湾北部两所大学和中部一所大学的372名大学生样本中进行了验证。初始量表包含49个项目,来源于定性访谈和文献综述。在内容效度评估和项目分析后,量表被精简为37个项目。对150名参与者进行了探索性因子分析(EFA),随后对222名参与者进行了验证性因子分析(CFA)。评估了同时效度和内部一致性信度。数据收集时间为2021年11月至2022年12月。结果:EFA的结果显示,一个双因素模型解释了总变异的60.30%,而CFA的结果表明拟合指标良好(GFI = .81,CFI = .92,TLI = .91,RMSEA = .07,SRMR = .02)。此外,两个因素的平均方差提取(AVE)分别为.56和.64,对应的组合信度(CR)值分别为.96和.93,表明具有良好的收敛效度。值得注意的是,AVE超过了相应因素的平方积矩相关系数,证实了良好的区分效度。最终的HPV-HLS量表包含21个项目,分为两个子量表:一般信息的理解与评估以及从医疗专业人员获取信息。该量表与中文多维健康素养问卷(r = .68,p < .001)之间表现出良好的同时效度。HPV-HLS表现出良好的内部一致性(Cronbach's α = .959)。结论/对未来实践的启示:经过严格的心理测量学测试,新开发的HPV-HLS表现出良好的信度和效度,使其成为评估大学生HPV预防健康素养的客观工具。该HPV-HLS可由医疗保健提供者和教育工作者用于评估健康素养,并评估预防HPV感染的临床干预措施的有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是一种常见的性传播病毒,与多种癌症有关。由于大学生属于高风险群体,了解他们关于HPV预防的健康素养至关重要。
设计与对象
采用横断面研究设计,该量表在台湾北部两所大学和中部一所大学的372名大学生样本中进行了验证。
核心方法
初始量表包含49个项目,来源于定性访谈和文献综述。在内容效度评估和项目分析后,量表被精简为37个项目。对150名参与者进行了探索性因子分析(EFA),随后对222名参与者进行了验证性因子分析(CFA)。评估了同时效度和内部一致性信度。
主要结果
EFA的结果显示,一个双因素模型解释了总变异的60.30%,而CFA的结果表明拟合指标良好(GFI = .81,CFI = .92,TLI = .91,RMSEA = .07,SRMR = .02)。此外,两个因素的平均方差提取(AVE)分别为.56和.64,对应的组合信度(CR)值分别为.96和.93,表明具有良好的收敛效度。值得注意的是,AVE超过了相应因素的平方积矩相关系数,证实了良好的区分效度。最终的HPV-HLS量表包含21个项目,分为两个子量表:一般信息的理解与评估以及从医疗专业人员获取信息。
解释与创新
经过严格的心理测量学测试,新开发的HPV-HLS表现出良好的信度和效度,使其成为评估大学生HPV预防健康素养的客观工具。
科研与公卫意义
该HPV-HLS可由医疗保健提供者和教育工作者用于评估健康素养,并评估预防HPV感染的临床干预措施的有效性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

Development and Psychometric Validation of the Human Papillomavirus Prevention Health Literacy Scale for College Students in Taiwan.

Authors: Ching-Yi Lai, Shu-Chen Yang, Chieh-Yu Liu, Hung-Ru Lin, Yuan-Mei Liao, Tzu-Ying Lee
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted virus associated with several types of cancer. Because college students are a high-risk group, understanding their health literacy with regard to HPV prevention is critical. However, no suitable measurement tools are currently available for this purpose. PURPOSE: In this study, an HPV Prevention Health Literacy Scale (HPV-HLS) was developed, and its reliability and validity were assessed. METHODS: A cross-sectional design was used, and the developed scale was validated on a sample of 372 college students from two universities in northern Taiwan and one university in central Taiwan. The initial scale comprised 49 items, developed from qualitative interviews and a literature review. After content validity assessment and item analysis, the scale was refined to 37 items. Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) was conducted with 150 participants, followed by confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) with 222 participants. Concurrent validity and internal consistency reliability were assessed. Data were collected from November 2021 to December 2022. RESULTS: The results of the EFA revealed a two-factor model accounted for 60.30% of the total variation, and those of the CFA demonstrated favorable goodness-of-fit indices (GFI = .81, CFI = .92, TLI = .91, RMSEA = .07, and SRMR = .02). Moreover, the average variance extracted (AVE) for the two factors were .56 and .64, respectively, with corresponding composite reliability (CR) values of .96 and .93, indicating excellent convergent validity. Notably, the AVE surpassed the squared product-moment correlation of the corresponding factors, affirming good discriminant validity. The final iteration of the HPV-HLS comprised 21 items with two subscales: comprehension and evaluation of general information and seeking information from health care professionals. Concurrent validity was found between the proposed scale and the Mandarin Multidimensional Health Literacy Questionnaire ( r = .68, p < .001). The HPV-HLS demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's α = .959). CONCLUSIONS/IMPLICATIONS FOR FUTURE PRACTICE: After rigorous psychometric testing, the newly developed HPV-HLS demonstrated excellent reliability and validity, establishing it as an objective tool for evaluating HPV prevention health literacy among college students. This HPV-HLS may be used by health care providers and educators to assess health literacy and evaluate the effectiveness of clinical interventions to prevent HPV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted virus associated with several types of cancer. Because college students are a high-risk group, understanding their health literacy with regard to HPV prevention is critical.
Design and population
A cross-sectional design was used, and the developed scale was validated on a sample of 372 college students from two universities in northern Taiwan and one university in central Taiwan.
Core methods
The initial scale comprised 49 items, developed from qualitative interviews and a literature review. After content validity assessment and item analysis, the scale was refined to 37 items. Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) was conducted with 150 participants, followed by confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) with 222 participants. Concurrent validity and internal consistency reliability were assessed.
Main results
The results of the EFA revealed a two-factor model accounted for 60.30% of the total variation, and those of the CFA demonstrated favorable goodness-of-fit indices (GFI = .81, CFI = .92, TLI = .91, RMSEA = .07, and SRMR = .02). Moreover, the average variance extracted (AVE) for the two factors were .56 and .64, respectively, with corresponding composite reliability (CR) values of .96 and .93, indicating excellent convergent validity. Notably, the AVE surpassed the squared product-moment correlation of the corresponding factors, affirming good discriminant validity. The final iteration of the HPV-HLS comprised 21 items with two subscales: comprehension and evaluation of general information and seeking information from health care professionals.
Interpretation and novelty
After rigorous psychometric testing, the newly developed HPV-HLS demonstrated excellent reliability and validity, establishing it as an objective tool for evaluating HPV prevention health literacy among college students.
Scientific and public-health significance
This HPV-HLS may be used by health care providers and educators to assess health literacy and evaluate the effectiveness of clinical interventions to prevent HPV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒 DNA检测的可接受性和偏好性与医生采集的巴氏涂片比较

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.
作者： Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
摘要中文翻译背景:自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。本研究旨在评估女性对自我采集阴道样本进行HPV DNA检测与巴氏涂片的感知、体验、意愿和偏好。方法:在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。使用五点李克特量表问卷在自我采集前后测量感知。采用描述性统计分析,并使用配对和独立Wilcoxon检验比较李克特量表回答。使用Cochran's Q和McNemar检验分析接受筛查的意愿。参与者偏好的筛查方法的原因被主题分类,并以堆叠条形图显示。结果:自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。结论:自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可以为提高HPV DNA检测参与率和扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性提供策略依据,强调了对自我采集方法进行教育和支持的必要性。局限性与证据强度:本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了关于可接受性和偏好的见解,但并未建立因果关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。
核心方法
感知通过五点李克特量表问卷在自我采样前和后进行测量。使用描述性统计,并采用配对和独立样本威尔科克森检验来比较李克特量表的回答。接受筛查的意愿通过科克兰Q检验和麦克纳马检验进行分析。
主要结果
自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。
解释与创新
自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能通过强调自我采样方法的教育和支持需求,为提高菲律宾HPV DNA检测的接受度并扩大筛查可及性提供策略依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于菲律宾的一个特定人群,未考虑长期依从性或结果。证据强度中等,因为其基于自我报告的感知和偏好。

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.

Authors: Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited. This study aimed to assess women's perceptions, experiences, willingness and preferences regarding self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing compared with Pap smears. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing. Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests. Participants' reasons for their preferred screening method were categorized thematically and displayed in stacked bar charts. RESULTS: Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness. CONCLUSIONS: Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing.
Core methods
Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests.
Main results
Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness.
Interpretation and novelty
Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies to improve the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines by highlighting the need for education and support for self-collection methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single population in the Philippines and does not account for long-term adherence or outcomes. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported perceptions and preferences.

槲皮素,邱华石汤剂的活性成分,通过调控PRKACA/cAMP/PKA/CREB通路抑制宫颈上皮细胞糖酵解以减少HPV感染。

Quercetin, an active component of Qiu's HuaShi decoction, suppresses glycolysis in cervical epithelial cells to reduce HPV infection via modulating the PRKACA/cAMP/PKA/CREB pathway.
作者： Mengting Xia, Haiou Xu, Nan Ding, Xinyan Wang
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌与高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的持续感染密切相关,并对女性健康构成重大威胁。邱华石汤剂(QHD)在宫颈癌 治疗中展现出潜在的治疗作用,但其作用机制尚不明确。方法:通过网络药理学筛选QHD针对HPV相关宫颈癌的核心靶点。通过整合体内和体外实验评估槲皮素的抗肿瘤效果。采用免疫荧光染色观察HPV16感染状态。同时,利用qRT-PCR和Western blot检测HPV16致癌基因E6和E7的mRNA和蛋白表达水平。免疫组织化学(IHC)用于动物组织中蛋白表达的原位验证。此外,通过Western blot定量分析PRKACA/PKA/CREB轴和核心糖酵解调节因子的关键分子蛋白水平。进一步,使用商业试剂盒评估cAMP含量、细胞葡萄糖摄取、乳酸产生以及细胞外酸化率(ECAR)和耗氧率(OCR)。此外,进行了PRKACA过表达实验以解析其在槲皮素介导效应中的调控作用。结果:网络药理学分析确定槲皮素和PRKACA为QHD对抗HPV相关宫颈癌的核心靶点。槲皮素抑制了早期HPV16感染,下调了HPV16 E6/E7的表达,并抑制了细胞增殖。机制上,槲皮素降低了细胞内cAMP水平,使cAMP/PKA/CREB信号轴失活,并抑制了关键糖酵解蛋白的表达。一致地,槲皮素抑制了糖酵解表型,包括葡萄糖摄取减少、乳酸分泌减少和ATP生成减少,同时伴随ECAR降低和OCR恢复。这些表型效应可通过PRKACA过表达逆转。结论:槲皮素通过抑制PRKACA表达来破坏cAMP/PKA/CREB/糖酵解信号轴,从而发挥其针对HPV相关宫颈癌的抗肿瘤作用,为QHD的临床应用提供了分子基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌与高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的持续感染密切相关,并对女性健康构成重大威胁。邱华石汤剂(QHD)在宫颈癌 治疗中展现出潜在的治疗作用,但其作用机制尚不明确。
设计与对象
通过整合体内和体外实验评估槲皮素的抗肿瘤效果。采用免疫荧光染色观察HPV16感染状态。同时,利用qRT-PCR和Western blot检测HPV16致癌基因E6和E7的mRNA和蛋白表达水平。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现为QHD在HPV相关宫颈癌的治疗中的临床应用提供了分子基础,可能有助于未来针对该疾病靶向治疗的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于摘要证据,可能缺乏详细的实验步骤和全面的数据分析。由于依赖体外和体内实验而没有长期临床结果,其证据强度中等。

Quercetin, an active component of Qiu's HuaShi decoction, suppresses glycolysis in cervical epithelial cells to reduce HPV infection via modulating the PRKACA/cAMP/PKA/CREB pathway.

Authors: Mengting Xia, Haiou Xu, Nan Ding, Xinyan Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer is strongly associated with persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) and poses a substantial threat to women's health. Qiu's HuaShi Decoction (QHD) exhibits potential in cervical cancer treatment, yet its underlying mechanism remains unclear. METHODS: Network pharmacology was utilized to screen the core targets of QHD against HPV-associated cervical cancer. The anti-tumor effects of quercetin were evaluated through integrated in vivo and in vitro experiments. Immunofluorescence staining was applied to observe the status of HPV16 infection. Meanwhile, qRT-PCR and western blot were used to detect the mRNA and protein expression levels of HPV16 oncogenes E6 and E7. Immunohistochemistry (IHC) was used for in situ validation of protein expression in animal tissues. In addition, western blot was performed to quantify the protein levels of key molecules involved in the PRKACA/PKA/CREB axis and core glycolytic regulators. Furthermore, cAMP content, cellular glucose uptake, lactate production and extracellular acidification rate (ECAR) and oxygen consumption rate (OCR) were assessed by commercial kits. Additionally, PRKACA overexpression experiments were performed to dissect the regulatory role of PRKACA in quercetin-mediated effects. RESULTS: Network pharmacology analysis identified quercetin and PRKACA as the core targets of QHD for combating HPV-associated cervical cancer. Quercetin inhibited early HPV16 infection, downregulated HPV16 E6/E7 expression, and suppressed cell proliferation. Mechanistically, quercetin reduced intracellular cAMP levels, inactivated the cAMP/PKA/CREB signaling axis, and suppressed the expression of key glycolytic proteins. Consistently, quercetin suppressed glycolytic phenotypes, including reduced glucose uptake, lactate secretion, and ATP production, accompanied by decreased ECAR and restored OCR in cervical epithelial cells. These phenotypic effects were reversed by PRKACA overexpression. CONCLUSIONS: Quercetin exerts its anti-tumor effects against HPV-related cervical cancer by inhibiting PRKACA expression to disrupt cAMP/PKA/CREB/glycolysis signaling axis, providing a molecular basis for the clinical application of QHD.
View research seven-element analysis
